二、主要稅目別變動 
（一）8 月實徵淨額與上年同月比較，以證券交易稅增加 262 億元、營業
稅、營利事業所得稅及綜合所得稅分別增加 68 億元、66 億元及 61
億元較多。 
（二）累計 1 至 8 月實徵淨額與上年同期比較，以證券交易稅增加 2,855
億元 、營利事業所得稅增加 1,599 億元及營業稅增加 906 億元較多。

TFT-LCD Panel Price Outlook
for Oct-26: TVs, Monitors and
NBs Flat MoM
While panel makers plan to raise the panel prices to mitigate
cost pressure, the coinciding softer seasonality in panel order
momentum makes this look a bit challenging, so negotiations
are undergoing. Contribution from class opportunities in
advanced packaging should only come in  2028 by earliest.

Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices to stay  flat MoM in October: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and 75"
TV panel prices are expected to stay flat for the month of October. While panel
makers had previously notified their brands customers of potential price hikes from
4Q26 to mitigate elevated component costs, we believe that negotiations are
ongoing, as TV panel order momentum might slow down in the coming months on

seasonality and TV brands could reduce orders on higher panel prices. On the other
hand, panel makers continue to demonstrate good discipline, since Chinese panel
makers are committed to annual maintenance during the October holiday to control
output. We think it seems more likely that we will see TV panel price hikes toward
late 4Q26 when TV brands start to restock for CNY promotions. Our base-case
assumption is for the TV panel prices  to be flat QoQ in 4Q26, on a quarterly average

basis.
IT panel prices to remain flat MoM in October: Monitor and notebook panel prices
are expected to be flat MoM during the month. Both applications continue to  see
slower demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about
increasing component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and
reluctant to given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect
IT panel prices to remain flat QoQ in 4Q26.

Risk/reward  less attractive: We maintain the view that the current valuation of
Taiwanese panel makers, AUO (2409.TW) at 2.0x P/B and Innolux (3481.TW) at 1.7x
P/B, have already baked in the expectations of glass in advanced packaging for
revenue contribution in 2028. However, some supply-chain players think this might
take longer, and there seem to be more suppliers from different supply chains vying
for this opportunity. In China, while expectations are also building for advanced

packaging, we have a    relative preference for BOE (000725.SZ; 1.4x P/B) over TCL
(000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B), on its scale  and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We believe current valuations of 1.5-2.0x P/B have already priced in expectations
of glass in advanced packaging from 2H28 for the panel makers.
Nonetheless,  the risk in commercialization and competition remain key to watch.
October 5, 2026 04:51 PM GMT

Note: To be more consistent with the industry practice, WitsView changed the way it
publishes panel price quotes in January 2025. It now only provides expectations of the full-
month panel price outlook on the 5th of every month, instead of specific quotes for the first
half of the month. It will then provide finalized prices for the month on the 20th of every
month, which will be similar to the original 2H panel pricing quotes.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
TV Panel Prices Trended Down in 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM, August was down 1% MoM, and September was flat MoM,   in line
with our expectation that TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That
said, we do not expect a significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide

downside support.

Below-seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization  ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance
following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.

Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, followed by a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing
structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to

profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural
Changes on Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In, June 17, 2021).

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Largely Fair
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply-chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.6x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end demand: If end demand for major applications turns out to be better
than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

## Document 079: 261006_daiwa_kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/261006_daiwa_kinsus.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We believe an improving product mix, driven by rising 
Nvidia’s CPU contribution, to be a key near-term share price catalyst.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. After Kinsus released its August 
preliminary results on 23 September, we note that we had underestimated 
the 3Q26E gross margin expansion on product optimisation and price hike 
scale. The results imply a GM of 33% in August (vs. 26% in 2Q26). We 
therefore lift our 3Q-4Q26E gross margin to 33.2-35.1% (from 28.9-30.3%) 
to reflect an improving product mix, including greater CPU substrate 
contribution. We believe the gross margin of high-end chipset substrate 
may be c.50%, supporting margin expansion. We also expect a c.10% QoQ 
price hike for both BT and ABF substrate in 3Q26, above Kinsus’ guidance 
of a high-single-digit% QoQ, making these substrates the other growth 
driver. We see further gross margin expansion in 2027-28 on the ongoing 
price hike trend and increasing high-end ABF substrate product mix.

Business outlook. We believe several favourable trends lie ahead. First, 
we see a rising contribution from high-end GPU and CPU ABF substrates. 
We believe Nvidia’s CPU currently accounts for 5-10% of total revenue, 
with Kinsus holding a 70-80% market share. We also expect Kinsus to 
acquire a teens% market share in AMD’s Venice, which should gradually 
ramp up from 4Q26. These will likely become key growth drivers of the ABF 
substrate segment in 2027, in our view. We believe the ramp-up progress 
for GPU substrate for these two clients is slightly slower than that for CPU. 
Second, we believe Kinsus will benefit from the rising BT substrate demand 
for optical transceivers. In our view, migration to on-board optics (OBO) and 
NPO/CPO will require ABF substrate beneath the optics engine (OE) in 
OBO; the OE and IC will need a bigger ABF substrate as we move to the 
NPO/CPO generation. As such, we expect the total ABF substrate demand 
to increase with rising adoption of optics in AI servers. Lastly, we expect the 
adoption of multiple-core ABF from 2028 to improve the rigidity and 
transmission performance before the official launch of ceramic and glass-
core substrate, which we expect to increase the ABF TAM as well.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E earnings by 19-29% to 
reflect the better-than-expected gross margin in August. We reaffirm our 
Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP to TWD1,580, based on an unchanged 
target PER of 36x (high end of 3-year-trading range of 10-36x), applied to 
the 1-year-forward EPS forecast. Key downside risks: weaker-than-
expected demand for memory, PCs, gaming GPUs or AI servers.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 27-57% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely on our more bullish view on its gross margin expansion.

6 October 2026

3Q26 results preview: several favourable trends ahead

➢ Rising high-end ABF substrate likely to drive blended ASP increase   
➢ Multicore and optics likely to be the next growth drivers 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12M TP to TWD1,580

Source: Daiwa forecasts

Company profile  
Kinsus was founded in 2000 as a subsidiary of Asustek. The company mainly focus on niche ball grid 
array (BGA) substrates to avoid price competition among mainstream products like graphics card and 
chipsets, and it has become top-3 flip chip manufacturer in global. Besides, its manufacturing sites are 
based in Taiwan and mainland China.

ESG analysis

Update Date: 31 Jan 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Kinsus's leadership team comprises experienced professionals dedicated to steering the
company towards sustainable growth. This diverse board composition ensures a comprehensive
range of expertise and perspectives, contributing to Kinsus’s strategic decision-making and
corporate governamce.
Capital management 2
Kinsus maintains a robust financial framework, ensuring the availability of resources necessary
for operational efficiency and strategic investments. The company's financial health is regularly
disclosed through annual reports, providing transparency to stakeholders.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company adheres to stringent corporate governance policies to manage related party
transactions, ensuring transparency and compliance with regulatory standards. The company
discloses such transactions in its financial reports, maintaining accountability and stakeholder
trust. For instance, the company's annual general meeting documents provide details on related
party transactions, ensuring transparency and accountability.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
Kinsus is committed to sustainable supply chain practices, emphasizing environmental, social,
and governance considerations. The company has established comprehensive ESG policies,
including commitments to labor standards, environmental health and safety, ethics, and conflict-
free minerals. These policies guide the company's interactions with suppliers and partners,
promoting sustainability throughout the supply chain.
E Data security 3
The company recognizes the importance of information security in its operations. As part of its
corporate governance framework, Kinsus is likely to implement standard data protection
practices to safeguard sensitive information and ensure compliance with relevant regulations.
Stakeholders are encouraged to consult the company's official communications or contact the
company directly for detailed information on data security policies.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

M
Update
Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
September  Sales  +22% MoM/
+206% YoY
In this report, we focus on Chroma's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• September revenue was NT$6,860mn (22% MoM/+206% YoY), a record
high.
• 3Q26 revenue came in at  NT$17,926mn (+32% QoQ/+180% YoY),
significantly higher than  our estimate at NT$14,772mn (+9% QoQ/+130%
YoY) and consensus at NT$14,555mn (+8% QoQ/+127% YoY).

Our view:
• We believe that the strength in 3Q26 was mainly driven by the
semiconductor and photonics business, especially SL T, metrology,
transceiver/CPO testing, etc.
• We think that Chroma might start to ship FT handlers and ovens in small
volume from late 2026/early 2027, which represent new business
opportunities for the coming years.
• Stay OW.

### Page 3

M
Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 45x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 45x is justified by the 61% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%) 과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%) 을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

## Document 213: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ October 12-16

Source PDF: all report pdf/ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ October 12-16.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-11
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: October 12-16
Date:    9 hours ago
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: October 12-16

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD:
October 12-16
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 11 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Monica Sauro / Unsplash

### Page 3

Last week was relatively quiet on the economic data front, but the
indicators released pointed to continued economic strength. ADP weekly
payroll gains increased for the fifth consecutive week, reaching a three-
month high, while jobless claims fell to multi-year lows. The ISM NM-PMI
remained solid at 54.9 in September, though its prices paid component
jumped to 74.0, the highest since July 2022. Redbook same-store sales
rose 8.3% y/y during the week of October 2, signaling continued strength

in consumer spending, while the New York Fed's consumer survey
showed expected spending growth and one-year-ahead inflation
expectations both reaching three-year highs.
The data suggest that the Fed has good reason to be hawkish.
Accordingly, September's FOMC meeting minutes showed that most
policymakers favored another rate hike by year-end, with several judging
monetary policy to be barely restrictive, if at all. We agree that further
tightening is warranted. However, recent comments by New York Fed

President John Williams, Fed Vice Chair Philip Jefferson, and Governor
Christopher Waller suggest flexibility on the timing. The markets assign a
17.2% probability to an October hike and a 71.6% probability of a
December hike.
This week's inflation reports could change those odds. A hotter-than-
expected CPI reading on Wednesday could prompt the markets to price in
an October hike, pressuring the Fed after September's unanimous 25bps
increase, which Fed Chair Kevin Warsh described as removing "a dose of

accommodation." An in-line or cooler CPI reading would support patience.
Warsh speaks Thursday evening at the IMF and World Bank annual
meetings in Bangkok.
Here's more:

### Page 4

(1) Inflation. The September CPI and PPI reports will be released on
Wednesday and Thursday, respectively. The Cleveland Fed’s Inflation
Nowcast model projects a hot 0.53% m/m increase in headline CPI, while
core CPI is expected to rise a more modest 0.2%. That would put headline
and core CPI inflation at 3.6% and 2.4% y/y, respectively. September is
expected to mark the 67th consecutive month of CPI inflation exceeding
2.0% y/y, underscoring the persistence of inflationary pressures (chart).

Adding to inflation concerns, the averages of the prices-paid and prices-
received indexes of five regional Fed banks’ business surveys rose to their
highest levels since 2022 in September (chart). Both have historically
tracked headline CPI inflation closely.

### Page 5

The same indicators have also closely tracked PPI final demand inflation,
which was already running hot at 5.4% y/y in August (chart).
(2) Monthly Treasury statement. The federal budget deficit totaled $1.77
trillion over the 12 months through August, while outstanding marketable
Treasury securities increased by $2.12 trillion over the 12 months through
September (chart). The recent slowdown in Treasury borrowing suggests
that the deficit may have narrowed somewhat. Tuesday's report will reveal

the final budget deficit for fiscal 2026, which ended on September 30.
(3) Consumer spending. Thursday's September retail sales report will
provide the first official reading on consumer spending for the month. We
expect another solid increase, as Redbook same-store sales rose 8.3%
y/y in September, well above the 2025 average of 5.8%. Redbook sales

### Page 6

historically have tracked official retail sales closely, suggesting that
consumer spending maintained strong momentum through the end of Q3
(chart).
(4) Manufacturing. The week will also provide important insights into the
manufacturing sector, with the October New York and Philadelphia Fed
banks’ business surveys due Thursday, followed by September's industrial
production and capacity utilization reports on Friday.
Manufacturing aggregate weekly hours rose to 84.4 in September, the

highest since August 2023, continuing their recent upward trend (chart).
Immediate expensing under the OBBBA, reshoring, and the AI buildout
are helping to reinvigorate manufacturing activity. With skilled labor in
short supply, manufacturers appear to be meeting stronger demand partly
by increasing the hours worked by existing employees. The rise in
aggregate hours worked suggests that manufacturing production
expanded at a healthy pace in September.

### Page 7

The New York and Philadelphia Fed manufacturing surveys provide
another reason for optimism. Their average manufacturing PMI rose to
22.7 in September, the highest since 2021 (chart). Historically, the
average has closely tracked manufacturing production growth, suggesting
that factory output could accelerate further in the months ahead.
💡

of the QuickTakes article. Paid members' contributions may be
featured in our segment, "Ed Answers Your Questions".

INFLATION TRACKER:
ENERGY PUSHES HEADLINE INFLATION HIGHER, CORE MEASURES
ARE STILL CONTAINED

### Page 2

Key takeaways
2ECONOMIC RESEARCH
1 A rise in inflation still confined to energy
Rising energy prices are pushing
inflation higher in Europe and the
United States, while energy’s
contribution is slightly negative in
Japan.
3 Mounting pressure on producer prices
2 Some recent pressure on core measures
4 Rising short-termmarket expectations
Markets (swaps) are pricing this
rise in inflation into short-term
measures. Long-term expectations
(5y5y) are rising in Japan and the

UK.
Over recent months (3m/3m), core
indices have been firming in the
euro area and Japan: a signal to
watch.
In Europe and Japan, pressure on
producer prices is building. The
risk of the energy shock spilling
over to other CPI items is
increasing.

## Document 282: Sales Trader Commentary_ Riding the TAIEX 50k Tailwind _ GSTW

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sales Trader Commentary_ Riding the TAIEX 50k Tailwind _ GSTW.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Sales Trader Commentary: Riding the TAIEX 50k
Tailwind | GSTW
6 October 2026 | 1:15 PM Taiwan Standard Time
Robert Wang
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. Taipei Branch
FICC & Equities
TAIEX futures officially touched 50k points for the first time, and the index is only
inches away. Local sentiment feels celebratory with higher level of participation, and
multiple catalysts coming up to edge the index higher. Global markets are reacting
well to the macro data (i.e. NFP on Friday). Here's what I am watching for the next two

weeks and some investor feedback:
Key Investor Feedback and Observation:
Major Event Coming Up. TSMC will report monthly revenue on Thursday and hold
earnings call on 10/15. We have raised our TP with a earnings preview note. We are
forecasting sequential growth into 4Q26 with slight GM dilution from N2 ramp. See
our full note here.
Flows and Positioning. Foreigners net bought US$2.3bn yesterday, the most in the
past 2 weeks and some clear rotation into large caps. TSMC returned as top bought

of close to US$800mn vs last week net outflows of US$500mn. From a monthly
perspective, Taiwan saw modest net buying alongside strong risk-on activity,
with hedge funds adding both long and short positions. Net allocation ended
the month at 7.2% (79%ile 1Y, 96%ile 5Y), while gross allocation finished at
4.5% (93%ile 1Y, 99%ile 5Y). See below exhibit for more details. Retail margin
balance kept refreshing record high until 10/5.
Secular Growth Sectors Catching Up. MLCC and Wafers broke out recently. Same

narrative - shortage, price hike, etc. When the expectation is low enough, it is easy
to trigger some quick inflows and technical rebound due to short covering. From
the fundamental perspective, components (i.e. Memory, MLCC, ABF, CPU) still have
supply constraint per Trendforce data. We can expect these companies to deliver
good top line and bottom line.
Some Long Ideas from the Desk.
Long TSMC Capex names - cleanroom to semi equipment should see some +ve

price action in the coming week. Top idea All Ring (6187 TT) on the advanced
packaging need with good order visibility from both TSMC and ASE.
Long Power - Delta (2308 TT) and Lite-On (2301 TT) should see secular
growth demand once 800/400VDC is deployed. Don't forget about fuel cells as
alternative energy sources - Buy Kaori (8996 TT)
Value Buy - long the underperformed hardware names such as Accton (2345
TT), Lotes (3533 TT), and GCE (2368 TT). Much better risk reward level at

below 20x Forward P/E valuations.
Watch Out for Volatility in High Price Stocks. We are seeing some profit taking in
recent outperformers such as Mediatek and GUC. Aggressive investors may even
10/6/26, 2:21 PM Sales Trader Commentary: Riding the TAIEX 50k Tailwind | GSTW
https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/6e9730b7-9d60-456a-8d36-151d2199ac2a 1/9

### Page 2

short into the strength.
Risk Management. A lot of investors are still worried about high treasury yield and
climbing oil price. Indeed, global market breadth is narrow; only AI complex trades
are outperforming. Fortunately, Taiwan is the top beneficiary of this whole AI
growth. Note that we have couple long weekends coming up in Taiwan, so investors
may take some chips off the table. Nonetheless, my view remains unchanged -
bright future ahead with upcoming earnings to back the momentum.

TPCA Show 2026. Taiwan annual PCB show is coming up on 10/20 - 22. Many
PCB/ABF players will participate at the show, while brokers will host events and
analyst meetings. Expect +VE catalyst in this sub sector.

The scenario, forecasts and nowcasts of the Economic Research

### Page 2

UNITED STATES 
The US economy is expected to grow above its potential in 2026, with an average annual growth rate of 2. 2%, close to the 2025 rate (2.1%) . 
This apparent resilience to the uncertainty, tariff and energy shocks, together with above-trend productivity growth, masks K-shaped growth, 
driven by investment linked to AI-optimism and consumption by the wealthiest amid a wealth effect (historically high stock market valuations). 
Inflation overshooting is set to continue (3.4% in 2026) at least through 2028, largely because of the rise in oil prices and tariffs – although the 
impact of these latter appears to be less significant than expected. The labour market is showing clear signs of improvement that should bring 
the unemployment rate down toward 4.0%. Given this shift in price and employment risks amid dynamic growth, the FOMC proceeded with a 
first rate hike at September’s meeting and we expect two further 25bp increases in December 2026 and January 2027, bringing the Fed Funds 
target range at 4,25% - 4,50%.

CHINA 
Economic growth slowed to 4.3% y/y in Q2 2026 vs. 5.0% in Q1. It is projected to reach 4.6% in 2026 as a whole, down from 5% in 2025. Growth 
remains characterized by a K-shaped trajectory, and the gap between the strong performance of exports and the fragility of sectors that rely 
on domestic demand has widened this year. Domestic demand has weakened significantly in recent months. Retail sales have barely increased, 
the crisis in the property sector has continued and household confidence remains low. Fiscal policy measures aimed at supporting investment 
and private consumption will increase over the last months of the year . CPI inflation has rebounded slightly since last March, mostly due to 
the rise in global energy prices, as well as due to the increase in electronic good prices and anti-involution measures implemented by the 
authorities. Deflationary pressures persist, however, given weakness in domestic demand.

EUROZONE 
Eurozone growth is expected to slow to 1.1% in 2026 (from 1.3% in 2025), before strengthening to 1.5% in 2027, supported by higher defence 
and infrastructure spending as well as the global AI capex cycle. The economy has proven resilient to the energy shock, notab ly thanks to the 
increasingly tangible impact of G erman defence spending on the real economy. Elevated energy prices in the autumn (especially for natural 
gas), combined with resilient growth, are set to accelerate inflation to a peak around the turn of the year. Annual average i nflation is thus 
expected to reach  3.1% in 2026 (from 2.1% in 2025), before easing to 3.0% in 2027. Given persistent inflation and resilient growth, we 
anticipate another 25bp hike from the ECB in December 2026, bringing the deposit rate to 2.75%, followed by an extended pause . That said, 
stronger than expected second-round effects could lead to more tightening than our current baseline.

Contacts

s
tephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com  
t
arik.rharrab@bnpparibas.com

### Page 3

FRANCE 
GDP growth remained low in Q2 (0% after -0.2% in Q1) as a result of deteriorated agricultural and construction output, as well as higher 
inflation (2.5% in 2026 according to our forecast, after 0.9% in 2025) . In 2026, GDP growth should  reach 0.5% (after 0.9% in 2025), driven 
mainly by exports.

UNITED KINGDOM 
UK GDP growth would improve slightly to 1.3% in 2026 and remain at this level in 2027  (1.2% in 2025). The economy has so far weathered 
both the energy price shock and domestic political volatility. That said, growth is expected to decelerate in the second half  of the year, while 
inflation is set to remain stubbornly above target (averaging 3.2% in both 2026 and 2027), driven by higher energy prices and mounting second-
round effects. In response, we expect the BoE to deliver a single insurance hike in November 2026, bringing the Bank Rate to 4.00%. The BoE 
is then expected to resume easing in H2 2027, with two cuts taking the Bank Rate to 3.50% by end-year.

JAPAN 
Following 1.2% in 2025, Japan's GDP growth is expected to decelerate to 1.0% in 2026, before rebounding to 1.2% in 2027. Higher inflation 
and production costs associated with the energy shock are expected to weigh on the economy’s overall performance. On the other hand, the 
latter is supported by fiscal policy  and AI-related investment. Inflation has generally overshot the 2% y/y target since 2022. Accordingly, the 
Bank of Japan initiated a cautious process of “adjustment in the degree of monetary accommodation” in 2024, lifting the policy rate to 1.25% 
so far (previously negative) – the highest since 1995. We expect two more hikes by the end of Q1 2027, followed by a return to a more cautious 
pace until the terminal rate of +2.5% is reached in H2 2028. Japan is facing long-term rates pressure, illustrated by historically high 10- and 30-
year yields, probably fueled by the level of public debt and the pace of monetary adjustment.

EXCHANGE RATES 
We expect Fed hikes to temporarily offset structural headwinds and support the dollar. EUR/USD would target 1.14 by end -2026, before the 
dollar's structural downtrend resumes toward 1.20 by end-2027, although political and fiscal risk may weigh on the euro next year. The USD/JPY 
appreciation would continue but at a more moderate pace, with the BoJ still behind the curve despite its hikes. By contrast, GBP/USD would 
see relative support underpinned by a modest fiscal risk premium under the new Labour government, and resilient growth.

## Document 302: The Wrap on Asia_ Asia’s goods disinflation over_ (2026-10-18)

Source PDF: all report pdf/The Wrap on Asia_ Asia’s goods disinflation over_ (2026-10-18).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-18
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
1
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WRAP ON ASIA
Mapping out Asia ...
Illiana Jain
Economist, Westpac Group
P: +61 403  908 032
E: illiana.jain@westpac.com.au
• For two decades, lower labour costs across Asia helped
suppress global goods inflation.
• Much of that disinflationary pulse originated in North Asia,

where production was increasingly offshored to lower-cost
labour markets.
• Today, ageing populations and rising incomes are lifting
labour costs across much of the region.
• While production continues to migrate towards South
and Southeast Asia, the next phase of manufacturing is
increasingly driven by capital, technology and power rather
than low-cost labour.
• As a result, labour costs are unlikely to provide the same
disinflationary impulse that supported goods prices over the

previous decade.
• Technology has historically provided a source of disinflation
as production costs fell and competition intensified.
• Asia sits at the centre of the AI supply chain through firms
such as TSMC, Samsung and SK Hynix, which produce
critical semiconductors and memory chips used in AI
infrastructure.
• Rising costs for electricity and critical minerals are increasing
production costs across the sector.
• Strong demand has allowed firms to pass these costs on

while maintaining healthy profitability. SK Hynix’s net profit
rose from ₩19.8tn to ₩42.9tn (+117%yr).
• Firms are also competing on performance and technological
capability rather than price, allowing technology-related
inflation to emerge for the first time in years.
• Many Asian economies remain reliant on imported energy,
leaving producers exposed to higher oil and LNG costs.
• Semiconductor manufacturing and the broader AI
infrastructure build-out are highly energy intensive.

Expansions by TSMC, Samsung and SK Hynix are increasing
electricity demand across North Asia while data-centre
investment adds a further source of demand.
• As technology firms compete with traditional manufacturers
for power, energy costs are becoming an increasingly
important part of the production process.
• Rising production costs are therefore being driven by both
higher energy prices and stronger underlying demand for
electricity.

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WRAP ON ASIA
• Chinese producer-price deflation remains evident across
parts of traditional manufacturing, particularly sectors
facing persistent overcapacity such as automobiles
• Historically, Chinese producer prices acted as the pricing
anchor for much of Asia’s manufacturing sector.

• What has changed is that parts of the region are increasingly
specialising in industries where China is not the dominant
supplier.
• Strong demand for semiconductors, memory and AI-related
goods is allowing firms in Taiwan and South Korea to set
prices according to their own market conditions despite
ongoing weakness in Chinese producer prices.
• China’s deflationary pulse remains important, but it no
longer guarantees lower prices across the rest of Asia.

• Rising costs were previously offset by production shifting to
lower-cost economies elsewhere in Asia.
• Production continues to migrate towards Vietnam, India,
Indonesia, Malaysia and Thailand, helping contain costs.
• However, these economies are unlikely to replicate the scale
of China’s integration into global manufacturing during the
2000s.
• High-value production remains concentrated in North
Asia while firms increasingly value resilience, security and

specialisation alongside cost minimisation.
• Future supply-chain shifts may therefore help limit
cost pressures, but are unlikely to generate the same
disinflationary pulse seen over the last two decades.
• For much of the past decade, falling goods prices helped
offset elevated services inflation across developed markets.
The forces that drove that disinflation are now becoming
less powerful.
• While Chinese deflation and production relocation to lower-

cost economies will continue to provide some support, they
are unlikely to fully offset these pressures.
• As a result, imported goods may provide less assistance in
bringing inflation back to target than they have in the past.
• This leaves central banks increasingly reliant on containing
domestic inflation pressures, particularly in services.
• As a result, achieving inflation targets may require a longer
period of restrictive monetary policy than has historically

been the case.

## Document 304: Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 1st October 2026

EcoFlash

Threats to European diesel imports: risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased 
risk of price rises

In Europe, the possibility of a US ban on diesel exports has heightened concerns about price trends for this widely used 
fuel1, which has already risen significantly since the outbreak of the conflict in Iran. It has become a major political issue 
on both sides of the Atlantic in the run-up to decisive elections.

A particularly tight diesel market due to a shortage of buffer stocks 
Following the outbreak of the conflict, diesel prices did indeed see some of the sharpest rises on the oil market due to particularly 
tight market conditions. Since the end of February, whilst the price of a barrel of crude has risen by 46%, diesel prices have soared 
by 100% in Europe and by 76% in the United States.

The factors alleviating pressure on the refined products market, and more specifically the diesel market, were more limited than in 
the case of crude oil. Shipments passing through the Strait of Hormuz (around 17% of maritime diesel trade prior to the conf lict) 
have fallen by around 75%. Added to this is the halt in Russian exports due to war damage, which is depriving the diesel market of 
around 12% of its maritime exports. Furthermore, unlike with crude oil, China has not acted as a stabilising  force in the diesel 
market. On the contrary, the energy crisis has led to a halt in Chinese exports of refined products (including diesel) to the  rest of 
Asia from April onwards. The resumption of Chinese exports over the last two months remains fragile, given, in particular, that 
diesel stocks are at their lowest level in 15 months.

The United States has eased these tensions in the diesel market by increasing its exports of refined products (up 25% on average 
in the first half of 2026 compared with the average over the last five years). This rise in exports has required high refinery utilisation 
rate (close to maximum capacity) and, to a lesser extent, a reduction in stocks (-11% compared with the average over the last five 
years as at mid -September for middle distillates 2). All these factors have contributed to tightening the US domestic market and 
pushed retail diesel prices to record levels. Road diesel prices (US average) have risen by 68% since the end of February and by 
74% year-on-year.

A structurally high level of vulnerability in Europe… 
Against this market backdrop, characterised by mounting tensions and very limited room for manoeuvre , Europe would be 
particularly hard hit by a reduction in – or even a complete halt to – US exports. According to Eurostat, imports accounted for 46% 
of European demand for diesel in the first half of 2026, with 40% of these imports coming from the United States. Europe, which

1 Diesel accounts for 17% of the primary energy mix in the EU and 7.3% in the United States. 
2 A category of refined petroleum products comprising mainly diesel and aviation fuel.

### Page 2

has lost a great deal of production capacity in recent years, is operating its refineries at near -maximum capacity. Its room for 
manoeuvre is therefore limited.

… But the risk of a shortage has been ruled out in the short term 
The risk of a shortage, however, appears limited in the short term. The situation regarding European stocks is not comfortable, but 
it is not alarming. Commercial diesel stocks in the ARA region 3 are currently at historically low levels (around 12 million barrels), 
23% below their level at the end of September 2025. As for emergency stocks (EU Emergency Stocks), these are, according to the 
European Commission, at a sufficient level. Eurostat estim ates this figure at 270 million barrels in June 2026 (stable from a year 
earlier), representing approximately 60 days’ worth of EU consumption. Europe imports around 0.9 mb/d of diesel from the United 
States, and emergency stocks would therefore cover around 60 days’ consumption at 4.5 mb/d.

The rise in prices could be significant 
Whatever the outcome of the conflict, diesel prices are likely to continue to rise in the short term. According to our base -case 
scenario, crude oil prices are expected to remain very high in Q4 2026 due to ongoing tensions in the Persian Gulf, whilst a return 
to normal levels of diesel exports from Russia and the Middle East appears unlikely by that time.

Among the factors likely to cause future tensions, the start of the maintenance season in Europe could affect production leve ls, 
whilst demand for diesel in the United States is typically high in the autumn (agricultural harvests and pre -winter storage). I n a 
scenario where tensions remain low, the diesel refining margin (crack spread) could rise by 5% in Q4 compared with its level at the 
end of September 2026, before gradually falling back during 2027. An intermediate scenario (a 10% rise in the crack spread in Q4 
2026) would result in moderate constraints on US diesel exports and a drawdown of European stocks. Finally, an adverse scenario 
(a 35% rise in the crack spread in Q4 2026) would imply severe restrictions on US diesel exports and a delayed European response.

What impact will this have on inflation in the euro area? 
According to our estimates, the rise in diesel prices would exacerbate the expected peak in inflation in the euro area without altering 
its timing. In our baseline scenario for September 2026 (see our recently published EcoPerspective), headline inflation would peak 
at 4.1% y/y in January 2027 before gradually falling back towards 2% by the end of the year. The two least adverse scenarios 
would add only a limited amount to the peak: +0.1 pp in the first scenario (+5% for the crack spread in Q4 compared with the end 
of Q3) and +0.2 pp in the intermediate scenario (+10%), with inflation reaching 4.2% and 4.3% respectively in January 2027. 
However, there is an upside risk in the second scenario linked to the uncertain extent of the spillov er effects. In both cases, the 
main impact would be felt through fuel prices (which quickly reflect rising market prices) and would fade over the course of 2027. 
In the case of the adverse scenario (+35%), the additional inflation would be close to 0.6 pp by early 2027, pushing inflation above 
4.6%. A shock of this magnitude would, in fact, reduce businesses’ ability to absorb rising costs, which would lead to price increases 
across a broader range of goods (food, industrial goods). Subsequently, the effect would only fade gradually.

Pascal Devaux 
Economist, Middle East, Western Balkans – Energy 
pascal.devaux@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe, Eurozone, International Trade 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

3 Amsterdam-Rotterdam-Antwerp, Europe’s main hub for the storage of raw materials.

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
“ 算⼒⾼铁 ” 是对⾼性能算⼒⽹的形象⽐喻。它通过⾼可靠、⾼质量、⾼速度的⽹络，把算
⼒、数据的供给⽅和需求⽅连接起来，实现算⼒资源的跨区域⾼速调度、⾼效协同与按需
取⽤，让算⼒像⾼铁乘客⼀样，安全、准时、⾼效地抵达每⼀个需求终点。⽂章指出，总
的来说，应遵循 “ ⾼速互联、⾼效调度、开放普惠、安全可靠 ” 总体原则，在实现全国⼤中型
城市基本覆盖的基础上，逐步将越来越多的地区纳⼊覆盖范围，让 “ 算⼒⾼铁 ” 像中国⾼铁⼀
样成为普惠型基础设施。
摘要：
近⽇，《求是》刊发中国⼯程院院⼠、紫⾦⼭实验室⾸席科学家刘韵洁⽂章《发展中国的 “ 算⼒⾼
铁 ” 》。
⽂章指出，当前，由⼈⼯智能引发的新⼀轮科技⾰命和产业变⾰加速演进，在此背景下算⼒如
同农业时代的⽔利、⼯业时代的电⼒⼀样，成为赋能千⾏百业的坚实底座。中国⾼铁，作为⼀

张享誉全球的 “ 国家名⽚ ” ，以其⾼速、可靠、准时、⽹络覆盖⼴泛⽽著称，成为⼤国⼯程与中国
智慧的代表。在算⼒成为数字经济发展核⼼要素、⼈⼯智能重塑全球竞争格局的今天，我们需
要建设覆盖全国、⾼效可靠的 “ 算⼒⾼铁 ” ，让它像中国⾼铁⼀样，成为⽀撑中国⼈⼯智能⾼质量
发展、抢占未来国际竞争制⾼点的国家利器。
“ 算⼒⾼铁 ” 是对⾼性能算⼒⽹的形象⽐喻。如果把分散在全国各地的算⼒中⼼⽐作 “ ⻋站 ” ， “ 算⼒
⾼铁 ” 就是连接全国所有算⼒站点、数据站点以及应⽤算⼒的单位和个⼈的 “ 数字⾼速铁路⽹ ” 。
它通过⾼可靠、⾼质量、⾼速度的⽹络，把算⼒、数据的供给⽅和需求⽅连接起来，实现算⼒
资源的跨区域⾼速调度、⾼效协同与按需取⽤，让算⼒像⾼铁乘客⼀样，安全、准时、⾼效地
抵达每⼀个需求终点。
那么，在技术条件具备的基础上，我们该怎样建设中国的 “ 算⼒⾼铁 ” 呢？
⽂章指出，总的来说，应遵循 “ ⾼速互联、⾼效调度、开放普惠、安全可靠 ” 总体原则，在实现全
国⼤中型城市基本覆盖的基础上，逐步将越来越多的地区纳⼊覆盖范围，让 “ 算⼒⾼铁 ” 像中国⾼

铁⼀样成为普惠型基础设施。⼀是落实顶层规划。贯彻落实国家关于算⼒并⽹⼯作部署的重要
举措，推动各地算⼒中⼼实现并⽹，加快建设全国⼀体化算⼒⽹。⼆是⽤好科技设施。⽬前，
我国信息通信领域⾸个国家重⼤科技基础设施 —— 未来⽹络试验设施（ CENI ），已构建起覆盖全
国 40 个城市核⼼节点、 133 个边缘节点的安全新⽹络，建设了东西部协同算⽹调度中⼼。在全国
算⼒ “ 池化 ” 的背景下，应⽤好这类设施，使其更好服务中国⼈⼯智能⾼质量发展。三是激发 “ 两
商 ” 活⼒。三⼤运营商是中国 “ 算⼒⾼铁 ” 建设的国家队和主⼒军，⼴⼤⽹络设备商是我国确定性
⽹络技术、光电融合技术取得成功的依靠⼒量，应⼤⼒⽀持这⽅⾯企业在⼈⼯智能领域加⼤研
发和创新⼒度，在 “ 算⼒⾼铁 ” 建设中发挥更⼤作⽤。四是强化资⾦⽀持。 “ 算⼒⾼铁 ” 是⼀个发展
前景⼗分⼴阔的新赛道，是中国智能经济、智能社会的新底座，在国家提出建设 “ 六张⽹ ” （⽔
⽹、新型电⽹、算⼒⽹、新⼀代通信⽹、城市地下管⽹、物流⽹）的背景下，应进⼀步强化⾦

融系统的⽀持⼒度，为 “ 算⼒⾼铁 ” 健康快速发展提供坚强保障。

均以四個月來最大幅度上漲。
從美伊雙方的態度來看 ，中東地緣僵局以及由此
帶來的能源價格壓力可能會持續一段時間 。歐元
區7月工業生產與零售銷售數據均較為疲軟 ，若能
源價格持續維持高檔 ，恐將推升企業成本並壓縮
家庭實質所得，對製造業與消費需求帶來壓力。
整體而言 ，歐元區經濟仍具韌性 ，但能源價格上
升所帶來的通膨與需求壓力 ，將是後續景氣的重
要觀察變數。

《歐洲央行9月會議如預期升息一碼，12月再次升
息可能性提高》
歐洲央行為了抑制通膨 ，2026年以來已升息二碼 ，
將存款機制利率上調至 2.5%。在歐央最新基準情
境預測中，2026、2027年歐元區GDP成長率預測
上修至0.9%、1.4%，然而，中東衝突持續、荷姆
茲海峽航運受阻及能源價格持續波動下 ，歐元區
經濟前景仍高度不確定。
通膨預估將在更長時間高於政策目標 ，基準情境
預估通膨率將在 4Q26升至3.6%高峰後，2Q27降
至2.5%。能源價格上升的影響將逐步傳導至非能
源價格 ，核心通膨預估 1Q27升至2.8%峰值後才
緩步回落，2027、2028年核心通膨率預測分別上

修0.1個百分點至 2.6%、2.3%，高於目標水準 。
不過，透過薪資傳導的二輪效應預期仍有限。
後續歐央政策將取決於能源價格衝擊的強度與持
續時間 ，以及是否進一步傳導至核心通膨 、薪資
與通膨預期。本行預期9月升息後，10月會議歐洲
央行將傾向觀望 ，然而，布蘭特原油期貨價格維
持在90美元/桶以上高檔 ，歐洲TTF天然氣期貨價
格漲幅更甚原油 ，在經濟維持韌性下 ，能源價格
維持高檔時間拉長 、核心通膨顯現能源價格的間
接傳導若增強 ，本行預估12月會議歐洲央行高機
率將再升息一碼，將存款機制利率升至2.75%。
資料來源：ECB，2026/9/10，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/9。

收更多公債供給，推升期限溢價，財政疑慮使部分

外溢影響，共同推升殖利率走高。

因素，中東僵局持續令能源價格維持高檔更久，除

差已跳升至 112.7個基點，創2012年6月以來新高 ，
主要反映市場對法國財政狀況的擔憂，今年第二季
法國政府債務佔GDP比重已逼近120%。

法國總理勒克努將於 10/1提出2027年預算案 ，法
國政府目標力拚節省540億歐元開支、將2027年財
政赤字佔GDP的比重降至5%。在政治局勢高度分
裂、2027年將舉行法國大選之際，此計劃將面臨強
大的政治阻力。無論是2027年預算討論、法國大選
民調的變化，相關不確定性預估將牽動短期內十年
期法德利差維持在約100個基點的高檔，無明顯回
落空間。

較易受到天然氣價格大漲的負面影響 ，且目前新

## Document 469: 华尔街开出中期选举价码：民主党因子暴涨20%，大盘却深陷“盲区”

Source PDF: all report pdf/华尔街开出中期选举价码：民主党因子暴涨20%，大盘却深陷“盲区”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-17
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

摩根⼤通构建的 " ⺠主党因⼦ " ⾃ 8 ⽉以来累计上涨约 20% ，⽽同期标普 500 仅涨 2.6% 。预测
平台对⺠主党横扫两院的概率已从 8 ⽉的 23% 飙升⾄ 64% 。⾼盛历史数据显⽰，该概率在投
票⽇前平均回落约 5 个百分点。机构分歧明显：摩根⼤通主张逢低买⼊，美国银⾏警告⺠主
党横扫将致股市下跌逾 10% 。
摘要：
华尔街已为即将到来的美国中期选举开出明确价码。
10 ⽉ 16 ⽇，知名财经分析平台 ZeroHedge 梳理各⼤投⾏最新研报指出，摩根⼤通构建的 “ ⺠主党
因⼦ ” ⾃ 8 ⽉以来累计上涨约 20% ，⽽同期标普 500 指数仅微涨 2.6% 。这种微观与宏观的严重背
离，正成为华尔街内部争论的核⼼焦点。
预测平台 Polymarket ⽬前对⺠主党横扫两院的概率定价已达 64% ⾄ 65% 左右；⾼盛综合  Kalshi
与  Polymarket 的平均预测数据，也在 10 ⽉ 8 ⽇前后触及  65% 的⾼位。
这⼀概率在仅两个⽉前的基⾦经理调查中还徘徊于 23% 的低位，如今却已跃升为市场的基准情

景。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

摩根⼤通 Delta-One 策略师 Manish Sinha 指出，如果将该因⼦的波动率拉满⾄与标普 500 指数相
同的⽔平，即按⻛险对等原则放⼤约 6 倍杠杆进⾏⽐较，其折算出的年化回报率⾼达约170%，
远超⼤盘同期约 15% 的表现。
值得注意的是，这⼀⾛势⼏乎完全由个股层⾯的公司特质因素驱动，与⼈⼯智能概念炒作、动
量交易、利率⾛势以及⼤盘系统性⻛险均⽆显著关联。
这意味着选举⻛险已被华尔街成功隔离并定义为多空对冲⼯具，以纯粹的超额收益形式在机构
间流通；反观美股⼤盘指数层⾯的定价，则依然停留在 “ 分裂国会 ” 这⼀旧有的基准情景之上。
“ ⺠主党因⼦ ” 的构建逻辑
据摩根⼤通 2026 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇发布的《美国选举系列 —— 中期选举》报告，该因⼦由 Delta-One 衍
⽣品策略师 Manish Sinha 主导构建。其核⼼框架最早搭建于 2024 年美国总统⼤选期间，此次针
对中期选举进⾏了重新启⽤与优化。
具体的构建⽅法是：对约 3000 只美股进⾏量化打分，衡量每只股票对预测平台  Polymarket 胜选

赔率变动的敏感程度，并赋予对应的 “ 选举得分 ” 。
随后将打分结果优化为两组流动性良好的多空对冲组合，共和党因⼦（ JP9REPP ）与⺠主党因
⼦（ JP9DEMP ）。
这两组因⼦在设计上均保持了对⼤盘⾛势的 “ 中性 ” ，旨在精准捕捉与选举直接相关的特质性价格
波动。
截⾄ 9 ⽉底，⺠主党因⼦累计上涨  19.45% ，对应  Polymarket 上⺠主党赢得参议院的概率突破
60% ；⽽共和党因⼦同期则下跌  14.57% 。
摩根⼤通的归因分析进⼀步揭⽰，该因⼦的绝⼤部分涨幅均来⾃股票⾃⾝的特质性成分，动
量、利率敏感度以及⼤盘系统性⻛险等传统⻛险因⼦对其⾛势⼏乎没有解释⼒。
Sinha 在 10 ⽉ 7 ⽇的报告中强调，该因⼦ “ 对⼤盘系统性⻛险、动量及其他宏观因⼦总体保持中
性 ” ，并将其列为捕捉战术性选举超额收益的核⼼⼯具。
赔率从 23% 飙升⾄ 64% ，但历史规律提⽰回调⻛险
驱动该因⼦⼀路⾼歌猛进的，是预测市场赔率的⼤幅迁移。

### Page 3

美国银⾏ 8 ⽉份发布的基⾦经理调查显⽰，当时仅有  23% 的受访者预期⺠主党会横扫两院，
47% 的受访者更看好 “ 共和党控制参议院、⺠主党控制众议院 ” 的分裂格局。
然⽽到了 9 ⽉ 4 ⽇， Kalshi 对横扫概率的定价已飙升⾄  47% ； 9 ⽉底⾄ 10 ⽉初，⾼盛测算的各⼤
平台平均胜率已达  65% 左右。
美国银⾏⾸席策略师 Michael Hartnett 在其最新⼀期资⾦流向周报中，也将 Polymarket 的横扫概
率记录为  64% ，较 9 ⽉的 49% 出现了显著抬升。
然⽽，⾼盛策略师 Alec Phillips 在 10 ⽉ 8 ⽇发布的《美国选举监测》报告中援引历史数据指出，
在过去历次中期选举（ 2002 年、 2006 年、 2010 年、 2018 年、 2022 年）中，预测市场在当前的
临近节点往往倾向于⾼估挑战党横扫两院的概率，从 10 ⽉初到最终投票⽇，该概率平均会有约 5
个百分点的回落。
如果将这⼀历史平均回落幅度应⽤于当前约  63% 的基准预测，⺠主党横扫的概率可能会回调⾄

50% 的中后段。
与此同时，参议院多数党席位的最终归属将取决于阿拉斯加、得克萨斯、俄亥俄、爱荷华和堪
萨斯等关键州。
⾼盛特别提醒，中期选举⺠调在偏⺠主党的州往往⾼估⺠主党，在偏共和党的州则⾼估共和
党，这与保守派选⺠ “ 选前集中归队 ” 的历史规律⾼度吻合。
美国有线电视新闻⽹（ CNN ）资深政治评论员 Harry Enten 也指出，共和党⼈在亚利桑那、内华
达、北卡罗来纳和宾⼣法尼亚四州的选⺠登记优势，已达到⾄少近 20 年来中期选举年的最佳⽔
平。
多空头⼨的具体指向
摩根⼤通的 Delta-One 团队在其代号为  JPMDC26P 的情景策略篮⼦中，详细梳理了 “ ⺠主党掌控
国会 ” 对各⼤⾏业的政策影响：
做多标的：受益于《平价医疗法案》保费税收抵免预期延续的管理式医疗与医院股，以及受
益于可负担性政策⽀持的低端消费股；

### Page 4

做空标的：中⼩市值国防股、资本市场相关股票（原因在于监管与调查将取代放松管制）、能
源许可证受益股，以及受《 CLARITY 法案》推动的加密货币相关标的。
加密货币板块的短期⾛势此前已提前演⽰了这⼀逻辑： 9 ⽉中旬参议院否决加密货币市场结构法
案时，所有⺠主党参议员均投出反对票，市场随即出现了剧烈下挫。
美国银⾏策略师 Hartnett 特对整体局势的判断更为直接。他认为，⺠主党横扫 “ 标志着⺠粹资本主
义向⺠粹社会主义的转向 ” ，意味着 “ 未来的税收与监管政策将总体向上⽽⾮向下，对企业每股收
益构成直接负⾯打击 ” 。
他推荐的⾸选对冲⼯具是做空⾦融股与美元—— 这与摩根⼤通因⼦中做空资本市场板块的策略
⽅向⾼度⼀致。
美股⼤盘：两⼤阵营的观点分歧
⾯对中期选举对美股⼤盘指数的影响，卖⽅机构分化为⽴场鲜明的两个阵营：
乐观派，以摩根⼤通的安德鲁 · 泰勒为代表。
泰勒认为，即便⺠主党横扫两院，也远未达到推翻总统否决权所需的三分之⼆绝对多数。实际
政治格局将类似于拜登执政最后两年、特朗普执政 1.0 时期最后两年或奥巴⻢执政最后两年 ——

本质上仍属于市场通常会正⾯响应的 “ 温和 / 权⼒制衡 ” 状态。
泰勒主张，如果选举前后股市出现回调，应当逢低买⼊。他援引历史数据指出：⾃ 1990 年以
来，标普 500 指数在中期选举⽇后的 3 个⽉、 6 个⽉、 9 个⽉和 12 个⽉内，平均涨幅分别为
5.4% 、 11.4% 、 12.5% 和  14.7% ；⾃ 1950 年以来的全部 19 次中期选举中，⼤盘在选举⽇后的 6

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
个⽉、 9 个⽉和 12 个⽉更是实现了 100% 的正回报。⾼盛同样将中期选举视为潜在的 “ ⻛险出清事
件 ” ，指出股市在选举前⼀个⽉的中位数跌幅为 3.5% ，⽽选举后四个⽉的中位数涨幅达  6.5% 。
审慎派，以美国银⾏的 Hartnett 特为代表。
在 Hartnett 特的情景预测中，⺠主党横扫将对应 “ 重⼤⻛险规避 ” 状态，届时美股可能下跌超过
10% ，美元与美债收益率同步⾛低，海外⾮美股市则相对跑赢。
⽽ “ 共和党控制参议院、⺠主党控制众议院 ” 的分裂格局才对应 “ ⻛险偏好上升 ”，届时 “ 国会分裂
实际上等同于适度增⻓的⾦发⼥孩状态，美股有望上涨约  5% 。共和党全⾯横扫则将是最⼤的正
⾯惊喜。
⾼盛定制篮⼦业务主管 Louis Miller 也警告称：

两个阵营分歧的根源在于：历史平均回报中包含了⼤量 “ 国会分裂 ” 的样本，⽽当前预测市场以
约三分之⼆概率定价的，恰恰是打破历史规律的 “ ⻛险规避 ” 情景。
然⽽⼤盘指数却对此视⽽不⻅ —— 年内累计上涨  13.4% 的⾏为模式表明，⼤盘依然在以 “ 国会分
裂 ” 作为基准进⾏定价。
真正的错误定价在指数，⽽⾮因⼦
zerohedge 认为，综合来看 “ ⺠主党因⼦ ” ⽬前已经变成了⼀笔相当拥挤的交易。它完成了⾃⼰的
历史使命，成功将胜率从 “ 掷硬币 ” 阶段的 20% 出头，货币化变现⾄ 64% 的基准情景；但要继续从
当前位置获利，难度正显著上升。
⾼盛的历史数据暗⽰胜率存在约 5 个百分点的⾃然回落空间，保守派倾向州的⺠调偏差也给参议
院预测带来了下⾏尾部⻛险，同时 Polymarket 等平台的赔率上涨空间已基本⻅顶。
相⽐之下，更显著的错误定价或许存在于⼤盘指数层⾯。如果 Hartnett 特关于 “ ⺠主党横扫将导致
股市回调超  10%” 的判断成⽴，⽽美股⼤盘⽬前定价的依然是和平⽆事的 “ 分裂国会 ” 情景，那么

当前最具性价⽐的对冲策略，显然是对⼤盘指数的下⾏⻛险进⾏保护（如买⼊指数看跌期权或
做空⾦融股），⽽⾮继续追⾼这个已经上涨了 20% 的主题因⼦。
距离 11 ⽉ 3 ⽇投票⽇，尚余约  22 天。
随着中期选举临近，选举结果很可能对⻛险资产不利。

南亞科 (2408 TT)

DRAM 明年仍緊缺

目標價上調至 660 元，潛在上漲空間+25% 
1. 3Q26 營收 825.49 億元，QoQ+68%；其中價漲料逾 6 成，優於富邦預期。 
2. 產業供不應求將持續，2027 年毛利率料逾 8 成。 
3. 市場正逐步拉高 DRAM 明年仍供不應求之預期，預料南亞科評價可望上調。

3Q26 價漲料逾 6 成，單季 EPS 估 26.22 元 
南亞科累積 7-9 月合併營收 1336.49 億元，QoQ+61.9%，YoY+611.7%，數字優
於富邦預期。由於公司銷售量大致 QoQ 持平，絕大部分季成長來自售價上揚；因
此我們預估本季毛利率約 87.0%，較前季上揚 7.5 個百分點。簡言之，我們預估南
亞科 3Q26 營業利益估約 1,102 億元，稅前淨利估 1,117 億元，歸屬母公司稅後淨
利估約 905 億元，EPS 估約 26.22 元。

DRAM 明年仍緊缺，價格修正料將延後 
市調機構及美光法說不約而同指出，DRAM 產業 2027 年供不應求將較今年更加嚴
峻，可見未來尚無法看到供需平衡的時間點。其次，南亞科 7 月法說已指出當時已
簽定之長短期 LTA 供貨合約佔總產能 50%，且下游客戶已依照 LTA 基礎價格低標
提供一定的預付款 。 無獨有偶 ，美光 10 月 1 日(台北時間)指出已簽署 26 份 SCA(策
略客戶協議)，目前協議涵蓋了約 2030 年以前約 35%的營收。簡言之，我們目前已
將 DRAM 價格修正的時點延後到 4Q27 或更晚。

產業供不應求將持續，27 年毛利率料逾 8 成 
展望 4Q26，我們預期 Consumer DRAM 漲幅約+3~8%、其他傳統 DRAM 漲幅
約 10~15%；4Q26EPS 估約 25.11 元，全年EPS 約 73.43 元。至於 2027 年，Fab 
5A(泰林新廠)預計 2H 開始有意義挹注產出；由於 DDR4 升級到 DDR5 的情況擴
大，銷售組合預料逐漸往 DDR5 放大，因此每 GB 平均售價可能因此出現 QoQ 下
滑。我們預估南亞科合併營收約 6,143 億元，YoY+56.2%；其中位元銷售量年增估
約二成 、ASP 年增約估三成 。簡言之 ，富邦預估南亞科 2027 年毛利率約達 86.3%，
營業利益估約 5,050 億元 ， 歸屬母公司稅後淨利估約4,163 億元 ，EPS 估約 120.66
元。

富邦不同之處 
富邦修正南亞科最新之 2026-27 年 EPS 預估值分別為 73.43、120.66 元，相較目
前 CMoney 同業平均預估值分別+10%、+12%，數字相對樂觀。

評價與風險 
我們預料美光 10 月 1 日法說後 ， 一部分投資人將逐步認同 DRAM 明年仍將供不應
求，售價也不會出現修正，南亞科股價預料將逐步墊高。簡言之，我們維持南亞科
買進的投資評等，目標價上調至 660 元(約 FY26 的 9.0PER 或 FY26 4.6XPBR)。南
亞科的股價風險來自(1)高階伺服器產品或私募股東合作進度不如預期、(2)AI 產業
展望出現意外的結構修正。

Rating Definition

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

## Document 508: 对冲基金今年收益已“蒸发一半”，近几周“亏损尤其惨重”，高盛合伙人：要等“垃圾级”资产止跌

Source PDF: all report pdf/对冲基金今年收益已“蒸发一半”，近几周“亏损尤其惨重”，高盛合伙人：要等“垃圾级”资产止跌.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-18
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

AI 资本结构底层的垃圾评级资产仍处于承压状态。在以 CoreWeave 为代表的垃圾评级 AI 资
产跌势未⽌之前，任何 AI 股票的反弹都只是仓位修复⾏情，⽽⾮基本⾯的重新定价。
摘要：
AI 交易逆转浪潮持续冲击市场，对冲基⾦仓位遭受重创，但⾼盛内部⼈⼠认为，抛售已⾛向极
端，钟摆或已摆得过远。
⾼盛 Prime 数据显⽰，亚洲基本⾯多空基⾦⽉内跌幅达 7% ，年初⾄今收益从 6 ⽉⾼点的 40% 骤降
⾄ 16% ，意味着过去四个⽉内已回吐逾半年收益，且⼤部分损失集中于近⼏周。全球 AI 股票连续
六个交易⽇遭净卖出，六⽇累计净流量 Z 值跌破 -3 ，为 ChatGPT 发布以来最极端的仓位削减幅
度。⾼盛分析师 Mark Wilson 指出，情绪、定价与仓位的动量均急剧转负，当前摆锤 " 极有可能已
向反⽅向摆得过远 " 。
与此同时，市场焦点正从 AI 需求端转向资本结构的信⽤⻛险。云计算公司 CoreWeave 股价单⽇
暴跌 15% ，五年期 CDS 触及历史⾼点，其 7 ⽉低点成为市场重点观察的关键技术位。Wilson 警

⽰，在以 CoreWeave 为代表的垃圾评级 AI 资产跌势未⽌之前，任何 AI 股票的反弹都只是仓位修
复⾏情，⽽⾮基本⾯的重新定价。
亏损图谱：收益回吐速度罕⻅
⾼盛 Prime 数据还原了此轮回撤的具体幅度。TMT 多空基⾦⽉内下跌 5% ，年初⾄今收益从 6 ⽉的
24% 收窄⾄ 12% ；全球基本⾯多空基⾦⽉内跌 2% ，年初⾄今收益从 17% 降⾄ 10% ；欧洲多空基
⾦⽉内持平，年初⾄今仍录得 8% 涨幅。
仓位层⾯的压⼒同样突出。⾼盛 Prime 账簿数据显⽰，全球 AI 股票（ GSXGGLAI ）连续六⽇遭净
卖出，六⽇累计净流量 Z 值跌破 -3 ，超过此前⼀周创下的 " 年内第⼆快抛售速度 " 纪录，并与量化
策略遭遇 8 ⽉以来最惨烈单⽇回撤同步发⽣。
Wilson 特别提及⼀项持续三年半的清晰交易逻辑 ——做多 "AI 赢家 " 、做空 "AI ⻛险资产 "—— 该
策略在年内⼀度斩获逾 90% 收益，但⾃ 6 ⽉底已近乎折半， 9 ⽉反弹尝试受阻后，当前正在重新

测试低点。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

双重催化剂：融资叙事的裂缝
Wilson 将本周最剧烈的波动归因于两个具体触发点。
其⼀， CoreWeave 联合创始⼈ McBee 在接受采访时称，寿险公司为 AI 提供 " ⽆上限 " 资本，即 " 全
世界的钱 "—— 这⼀表态引发市场对私⼈信贷定价逻辑的重新审视，也令外界对 AI 融资链条中最
薄弱环节的担忧浮出⽔⾯。
其⼆，围绕前沿 AI ⼤模型商业模式与经济学的辩论趋于激烈 —— 包括 OpenAI ⾸席财务官 Sarah
Friar 公开警告企业慎⽤廉价开源模型。Wilson 预计这⼀辩论将在 " 未来数周内更加清晰透明 " ，
并将直接影响市场对资本⽀出规模、信⽤质量以及 AI 价值归属的判断。
两条线索背后，指向同⼀个核⼼问题：AI 资本堆叠中，谁来为⾼额融资成本买单？⾼盛信⽤策
略师将 " 新兴云服务商及前沿实验室相关 AI 公司债券 " 定性为 " 融资成本辩论的震中 " 。
多头论点：需求端尚⽆放缓证据
Wilson 并不认同全⾯看空的⽴场。他承认资本⽀出与盈利增⻓的 " ⼆阶导数 " ⼤概率将从当前⽔平

放缓，但强调价格已在相当程度上反映了这⼀预期。
其核⼼论据来⾃ OpenAI CFO Sarah Friar 约⼀个⽉前在⾼盛会议上的发⾔： " 过去两年因过度投
⼊算⼒⽽饱受批评，但今年完全值得。我⼀年前购⼊的算⼒，今天在市场上能卖出 3 ⾄ 5 倍的价
格。唯⼀的坏消息是，我们算⼒还是不够，应该买得更多。"
Wilson 据此重申其核⼼判断：市场领导权将从 "AI ⽀出受益者 " 切换⾄ " 最⼤ AI 投资⽅ "—— 即正
在通过三季度财报兑现投资回报的超⼤型科技公司。他认为，这批公司⼤多能够⾃⾏⽀撑资本
开⽀，相对不受价值归属争议的冲击，且其股价已开始跑赢更⼴泛的 AI 赢家篮⼦。

### Page 3

债券信号与供给前景
Wilson 指出，本轮市场调整中⼀个值得关注的变化发⽣在债券市场。
尽管债券⾛强的驱动因素 —— 增⻓放缓⻛险及融资意外 —— 并⾮股票投资者所期待的那种，但
10 年期美债收益率在 CPI 与 PPI 数据公布后未能进⼀步上⾏，并于周四明显回落，突破陡峭上⾏
趋势线。 Wilson 认为，这 " 体现出此前从未出现的某种疲态 " 。
不过 Wilson 同时警告，能源价格压⼒尚未出现类似缓解， 12 ⽉ WTI 期货逼近 100 美元，利率⻛险
仍不可低估。
展望供给端，⾼盛 Ben Snider 估计，包含到期锁定期在内，美国公开市场股票净供给明年将创
2000 年以来最⼤增幅。 Wilson 对此相对淡定，认为历史数据显⽰，只要市场相信股权融资⽤于
⽣产性投⼊，较⾼的供给⽔平本⾝并不构成指数上涨的障碍 —— 关键在于 " 市场是否相信这⼀
点 " 。
核⼼关注：垃圾评级资产是否⽌跌
Wilson 的多头论据建⽴在三个⽀柱上：仓位已被充分洗出、债券趋于稳定、算⼒需求端⽆放缓

迹象。
然⽽关键⽭盾在于，市场当前追问的问题已不再是 " 算⼒需求是否存在 " ，⽽是 " 谁来为之付款 " 。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

ユーロ圏景気概況① 景気は拡大している
ユーロ圏の4-6月期の実質GDP（確定値）は前期比+0.6%と、1-3月期（同横ばい）から反発した。需要項目別には、前期
にマイナス寄与だった純輸出が大幅なプラス寄与に転じて景気をけん引した。一方、最新9月の景況感指数は97.9と低下
に転じた。酷暑によるさまざまな調整圧力や政情不安（特にフランス）が景気の重荷になっているもよう。

ユーロ圏景気概況② 生産は低迷している
ユーロ圏の6月の鉱工業生産は前月比▲0.1%と2カ月連続で減少し、均した動きでも下向きに。非耐久財が減産に転じ
て生産全体の重荷となった。一方、同月の名目輸出は同+1.2%と増勢が前月（同+0.7%）から加速し、均した動きでも上
向き。ゆえに生産の低迷は内需向けが主因であるもよう。

ら年2.5%へ引き上げた。3%近いインフレが定着している一方、景気が底堅いことが背景。次々回の理事会の結果は10

支（貿易）黒字が高止まりしており、実需面からのユーロ買い圧力は安定している。

景気は底堅い
（出所）米商務省 （出所）米供給管理協会
米国経済は堅調に推移している。4-6月期の実質GDP成長率は前期比年率2.2%に鈍化した。政府支出と在庫投資が減
少し成長率を押し下げたが、個人消費、設備投資は底堅く推移し、民間最終需要はおよそ3年ぶりの高い伸びを記録した。
企業の景況感を示すISM景況指数は、製造業、非製造業いずれも判断の目安となる50の水準を上回った。先行き、堅調
な個人消費やAI需要の一段の高まりなどから景気は底堅く推移する見込みである。

### Page 6

5
Mitsubishi UFJ Research and Consulting
LINE 本文ページ
AI関連投資と金利上昇
◼ コロンビア大のニューワーバーグ教授が9月23日ブルッキングス研究所で公表したAIに関する論文が注目されている。
論文では、データセンターを中心にAIインフラ整備の投資額は、2032年にかけ年平均対GDP比3.63%にのぼるとする。
これは、鉄道、高速道路など過去のインフラ整備のための投資と比べ規模が格段に大きい。
◼ これだけの巨額投資が実施されれば、米国の潜在成長率は上昇する。最近の長期金利上昇要因をみると、期待インフ
レ率やタームプレミアムがほぼ横ばいにとどまる中、実質金利を示すインフレ連動国債利回りが上昇している。こうした
こともあり、足元の長期金利上昇について、良い金利上昇との前向きな解釈が一部で指摘されている。
長期金利の要因分解インフラ整備のための投資規模（年平均、対GDP比、%）
運河 1836-41 0.66
鉄道 1870-90 2.24
電力網 1905-25 0.50

高速道路 1956-73 1.13

FOMC委員の経済金融見通し（18人の委員による予測の中央値）
◼ FRBは9月15、16日に開催した連邦公開市場委員会（FOMC）で、政策金利であるFF金利の誘導目標を3.75～4.00%
に引き上げた。 利上げは2023年7月以来およそ3年ぶりとなる。ウォーシュ議長は会見で、インフレ率が高すぎる状態
が長引いていると述べた。
◼ FOMC委員の経済金融見通しは、景気の底堅さとインフレの高止まりを踏まえ、26、27年の成長率予測と、26年の物
価見通しを上方修正した。そのうえで金融政策については今年あと1回の利上げが想定されている。
◼ 米金利先物からFOMC会合ごとの政策金利を予想するCMEの「フェドウオッチ」によると、26、27年にかけて3回程度の
利上げが織り込まれている。実際の金利変更の有無やその時期が今後の焦点となる。

## Document 580: 米国：9月雇用統計はFRBに時間的猶予を与える内容 ～10月FOMC利上げ観測後退、トランプ2.0下での労働市場の「綱渡り均衡」が継続～

米国：9 月雇用統計は FRB に時間的猶予を与える内容 
～10 月 FOMC 利上げ観測後退、トランプ 2.0 下での労働市場の「綱渡り均衡」が継続～ 
 経済調査部 主任エコノミスト  桂畑 誠治  
（℡：050-5474-7493）

要旨 
・2026 年 9 月の非農業部門雇用者数は前月差+2.9 万人と予想を下回り、過去分の下方修正も 
相まって雇用のモメンタムは鈍化した。医療・建設・飲食店などの内需関連が全体を下支えする 
一方、派遣やAI・構造改革の影響を受ける金融・IT、政策的不確実性を抱える一部製造業では人
員削減が進むなど、業種間の二極化が鮮明化している。 
・失業率は 4.2%と低水準を維持している。トランプ政権下の政策不確実性による採用抑制や、 
移民規制の強化に伴う供給制約を背景に、労働市場は低雇用と低解雇が同居する膠着した均衡状 
態にある。労働供給が抑えられているため、雇用の伸びが鈍化しても失業率は上がりづらく、タ 
イトな需給バランスが続いている。 
・平均時給の伸びは前年比+3.0%へと緩やかな鈍化傾向が続いている。一方、ガソリン価格の上 
昇などインフレの再燃の影響で実質賃金はマイナス圏にとどまっており、名目総労働所得の伸び 
悩みと併せて、今後の個人消費の重石となる可能性がある。 
・予想を下回る雇用統計を受け、市場では次回 10 月FOMCでの政策金利据え置き観測が強ま 
り、米長期金利低下・ドル安・株高の反応となった。基本的には労働市場の安定が続いているた
め、FRBの今後の政策はインフレ動向への依存が強く、インフレが高止まりすれば追加利上げ、 
緩やかな低下にとどまれば金利据え置きとなる公算が大きい。

（千人）
（出所）米労働省より作成
1. 非農業部門雇用者数は増加ペース減速
2026 年 9 月の非農業部門雇用者数（事業所調査）は、前月差+2.9 万人（前月改定値:同+13.3 万
人）と、市場予想中央値（ブルームバーグ集計）の同+9.0 万人および筆者予想の+9.8 万人を下回
った。内訳をみると、政府部門が前月差▲1.7 万人（前月改定値:同+4.4 万人）と減少に転じたほ
か、民間部門は同+4.6 万人（前月改定値:同+8.9 万人）と伸びが減速した。医療・建設分野が底
堅さを維持したものの、飲食店など前月に高い伸びとなった業態で勢いが鈍化した。

雇用の基調を示す移動平均をみると、非農業部門雇用者数の 3 カ月移動平均は前月差+5.1 万人
（前月:同+5.1 万人）と横ばいとなったものの、6 カ月移動平均では同+6.6 万人（前月:同+9.7 万
人）へ減速した。これは当月の減速に加え、8 月分が+13.3 万人（速報値＋16.2 万人） 、7 月分が
▲1.0 万人（速報値+2.1 万人）と、過去 2 カ月分で計 6.0 万人下方修正された影響が大きい。過
去分の改定を含めた雇用モメンタムは再び減速傾向を示している。
民間部門に限っても、3 カ月移動平均こそ前月差+5.4 万人（前月:+4.8 万人）と小幅に加速した
が、6 カ月移動平均では同+6.7 万人（前月:+9.3 万人）と鈍化した。単月ではイベントや天候要
因、ストライキなどの一時的なノイズが見られるものの、雇用増加のモメンタム低下は明確であ
る。もっとも、急激な悪化は回避されており、横ばい圏に向けた緩やかな減速にとどまってい
る。
また、後述の失業率（家計調査）が小幅上昇したものの 4％台前半という低い水準を維持してい

ることなどを踏まえれば、労働市場全体の安定性は保たれていると判断される。

2. 民間部門では、医療・社会扶助、飲食店が減速しつつも全体を下支え 
詳細な内訳をみると、政府部門では連邦政府が前月差▲0.1 万人（前月改定値:同▲0.3 万人） 
と減少を続けたことに加えて、州・地方政府が教員数の減少を受けて同▲1.6 万人（前月改定値:
同+4.7 万人）と減少に転じ、政府全体を押し下げた。 
民間部門では、高齢化に伴う構造的な需要増と人手不足が続く医療・社会扶助（+2.30 万人）が
もっとも増加した。また、生成AI需要の拡大に伴うデータセンターの建設ラッシュやインフラ投 
資を背景とした建設（+1.10 万人）や飲食店（+1.08 万人）が底堅さを示した。このほか、一般機 
械、輸送機器、プラスチック・ゴムなどの製造業（+0.90 万人） 、輸送・倉庫（+0.76 万人） 、専
門・技術サービス（+0.64 万人） 、その他サービス（+0.60 万人） 、小売（+0.58 万人） 、卸売 
（+0.50 万人）が増加した。加えて、芸術・エンターテインメント・余暇（+0.25 万人） 、公益 
（+0.05 万人） 、不動産・リース（＋0.05 万人）等でも雇用が拡大した。 
対照的に、労働市場の先行指標とされる派遣（▲1.09 万人）が再び減少に転じており、今後の 
雇用の伸び鈍化を示唆している。このほか、宿泊（▲0.36 万人） 、教育サービス（▲0.32 万人） 、
政府補助金削減の影響を受けた保険（▲0.23 万人）や、AI導入および構造改革の影響を受けてい 
る情報（▲1.00 万人） 、商業銀行（▲0.05 万人）が減少した。このほか、鉱業（▲0.20 万人）な
どもマイナスとなった。 
こうした業種別の動向からは、労働市場の二極化が鮮明に読み取れる。医療・社会扶助や建設 
が旺盛な労働需要を吸収する一方で、生成AIの普及に伴う構造変化に直面する金融・IT、ならび 
に通商政策をはじめとする政策的不確実性を抱える一部の製造業では適正化に向けた人員調整が
継続している。

3. 金融市場では 10 月の利上げ見送り観測が強まる 
予想を下回る雇用指標を受け、金融市場では次回 10 月FOMCでの政策金利据え置き観測が強まっ
た。FF金利先物市場が織り込む確率推移をみると、まず 10 月FOMCでの据え置きの確率は約 82％
（前日:約 76％）へ上昇し、25bpの利上げ確率は約 18％（前日:約 24％）へ低下した。 
12 月FOMC時点で現行水準（3.75～4.00％）に留まる確率は約 25％（前日:約 20％）へ上昇し、
年内 25bp以上の利上げ確率は約 75％（前日:約 80％）へ低下した。2026 年末のFF金利の着地予想 
も 3.93％（前日:3.94％）へと小幅に低下した。 
金利先物市場の動きを反映した金融市場の初期反応では、労働市場の減速を織り込む形で米 2
年国債および 10 年国債の利回りは低下した。為替市場ではドルが主要通貨に対して弱含み（ドル 
安）で推移した一方、株式市場では過度な金融引き締めへの警戒感が和らいだことで、主要 3 指
数がそろって上昇した。

4. 労働市場はトランプ 2.0 がもたらす「低雇用・低解雇」の膠着均衡 
労働市場変容の背景には、トランプ 2.0 政権下の政策バイアスが色濃く影を落としている。相
次ぐ大統領令による制度や規制の急進的な変更が現場の混乱を招き、2025 年以降の米労働市場は 
基調として軟化傾向にあった。特に通商政策における関税の賦課・撤回・上乗せを巡る不確実性 
が企業のセンチメントを冷やし、採用の抑制や局所的な人員調整を誘発している。 
また、移民規制の厳格化や不法移民の取り締まり強化が、労働供給の直接的な抑制に繋がって
いる点も重要である。JOLTS（労働異動調査）における求人倍率や採用率の低下、ならびにレイオ

### Page 5

3000 
5000

(千人） 
米国雇用情勢 
採用者数 
自己都合による退職者 
ﾚｲｵﾌ・解雇 
（出所）米労働省より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成 
フ率の歴史的低水準が示す通り、現在の米労働市場は構造的な供給制約を背景に、企業の採用抑 
制（低雇用）と、人手不足に伴う解雇の抑制（低解雇）が同居する「一種の引き締まった均衡状
態」にあると言える。これは労働市場の流動性が著しく低下していることを意味する。

5. 賃金の伸びは鈍化傾向、インフレ再燃で実質賃金はマイナス圏 
9 月の平均時給は前月比+0.1％（前月:+0.3％）となり、市場予想（+0.3％）を下回った。前年 
同月比でも+3.0％（前月:+3.1％）と市場予想（+3.1％）を下回り、2022 年 3 月のピーク 
（+5.9％）からの緩やかな低下傾向は維持されている。しかし、トランプ関税の影響やイラン・
中東情勢の緊迫化に伴うガソリン価格の上昇で、消費者物価が再上昇しているため、名目賃金の 
伸び鈍化と相まって実質賃金（前年比）は小幅ながらマイナス圏にとどまっている。今後の個人 
消費の下押しリスクとして注視が必要である。 
一方、民間部門の週平均労働時間は 34.3 時間（前月比 0.0％）と横ばいにとどまり、労働投入 
量（雇用者数×総労働時間）は前月比 0.0％（前月:+0.4％）と減速した。もっとも、3 カ月移動 
平均・3 カ月前対比年率では+1.3％（前月:+1.3％）と堅調さを維持しており、全体としての労働 
需要の底堅さを示している。 
購買力の源泉となる名目総労働所得（＝平均時給×総労働時間×雇用者数）は、前月比+0.2％
程度（前月改定値:+0.5％程度）へと鈍化した。前年同月比でも+3.9％程度（前月改定値:+4.0％
程度）と緩やかな減速傾向にある。雇用者数の伸び抑制と時給の伸び率の低下が重なっており、
インフレ再燃（ガソリン高など）も加わることで実質総所得の伸びがさらに押し下げられてお 
り、今後の個人消費を抑制する要因となる可能性がある。

6．失業率は 4.2％と小幅上昇も低水準を維持、労働需給のタイトさは継続 
家計調査によると、9 月の失業率（U3）は 4.2％（前月:4.1％）と 0.1 ポイント上昇し、市場予 
想中央値（4.1％）を上回った。求職活動の再開等に伴い労働参加率が 61.8％（前月:61.6％）へ
と上昇したことが失業率の押し上げ要因となっているが、依然として歴史的な低い水準にある。
一方、 「現在は職探しをしていないが過去１年間に求職活動を行った人」と「正規雇用を探してい 
るが経済的理由によりパートタイムで働いている人」を含む「広義の失業率（U6） 」は、7.6％
（前月:7.7％）へ 0.1 ポイント低下しており、不完全雇用の拡大には抑制がかかっている。  
また、労働者のマインドを示す「失業者に占める自発的離職者の割合」は 10.4％（前月: 
13.0％）へと大幅に低下（▲2.6 ポイント）した。労働者が転職市場の冷え込みを察知し、自主的 
な離職を控えるリスク回避姿勢を強めている動きが窺える。 
労働参加率が過去の高水準に戻らない構造的要因として、トランプ政権による移民流入の抑制 
に加え、団塊世代の退職加速が挙げられる。労働供給が物理的に制約される環境下では、雇用者 
数の伸びが鈍化したとしても失業率は上昇しにくく、タイトな労働需給の均衡状態が当面続く見 
通しだ。

7．総括：FRBはインフレ動向次第で追加利上げも選択肢に 
9 月の雇用統計は、 「雇用増加ペースの鈍化」と「失業率の低水準推移」が共存する結果となっ 
た。これは、政策由来の構造的な供給制約と、サービス業や建設投資に支えられた底堅い労働需 
要が拮抗する「綱渡りの均衡状態」を反映している。 
FRBにとっては、労働市場が過熱も急悪化もせずバランスを保っていることが確認できた。この 
ため、今後の金融政策運営の軸足は、労働市場の冷え込みへの配慮よりもインフレの動向へとシ 
フトしたままである。インフレの低下が鈍ければ追加利上げが視野に入る一方、金利高の影響で 
景気が減速しインフレの緩やかな低下が続けば、政策金利を据え置く展開が予想される。

### Page 8

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。

Lily.tsai@ctbcsis.com

紡織  
聚陽 (1477 TT)

維持評等  
增加持股(Overweight) 
4Q26 營運展望正向 ；訂單篩選策略啟動  
⚫ 3Q26 成長動能 延續 ；管理層 審慎 樂觀展望 4Q26  
⚫ 下單模式 轉變；客戶與 訂單篩選支 撐獲利能力  
⚫ 2027 年復甦幅 度仍取決於客戶補 庫存時點  
營運維持溫和復甦 ，4Q26 展望轉趨正向 ：聚陽 3Q26 營收 94.8 億元 、
YoY+6.7% ；前 9 月累計營收 由負轉正 為年增 0.2% ，整體出貨大致符
合公司預期 。9 月營收 29.3 億元、 YoY+24.1% ，因去年同期 低基期 而
有較高成長 ，但適逢聖誕節與感恩節出貨旺季導致船班擁擠，約 8,000
萬元營收遞延 至次月 。3Q26 出貨量 估維持個位數年增， ASP 大致持
平，預期毛利率仍可維持 24% 以上水準。展望 4Q26 ，管理層看法轉
趨正向，並啟動訂單篩選，包含 挑選 客戶、產品類別及 提高 短單比重 ，
優先承接獲利能力較佳的業務，預期將有助於提升獲利表現。  
短單占比提升，客戶 與訂單 篩選支撐獲利能力 ：受中東戰 爭、油價及
終端需求 不確定性影響，客戶下單模式 轉變， 初期訂單通常未一次下
滿，後續再以追加單補足 ，此趨勢預計延續至 4Q26。客戶下單模式轉
向短單 ，使訂單能見度 維持在約 4 個月 ，但聚陽具備快速反應與急短
單生產優勢， 而短單報價相對較佳，反而有利毛利率表現。公司將篩
選價格及獲利條件較佳的訂單，減少關稅與原物料成本壓力較大的品
項；4Q26 成長 動能主要來自百貨公司、美系品牌及通路商回補庫存 需
求，在訂單 結構及產品組合優化帶動下，獲利能力有望持續改善 。 
東南亞持續擴產， 2027 年復甦幅度仍取決於 客戶 補庫存時點 ：產能 佈
局方面 ，由於 菲律賓廠 關閉 抵銷部分新增產能， 2026 年整體 產能增幅
有限，未來將持續以印尼與孟加拉 作為主要 擴產 據點。展望 2027 年，
客戶庫存已處低檔，管理層認為待美國期中選舉結束、政策與外部環
境明朗後，客戶 下單週期有望逐步恢復正常 ，將對具規模的 大型 供應
商相對有利。不過，目前終端需求仍屬 溫和 ，回補庫存啟動時點及力
道尚未明確，因此短期營運可望延續 緩和 改善 趨勢 。公司表 示內部 營
運體質已 完成 調整，後續較明顯的成長仍須等待外部需求回溫。  
4Q26 營運正向， 2027 年也有望持續復甦 ，惟 時程與 幅度仍須觀察 。
因此維持審慎的 14 倍 PER ，以未來四季預估 EPS 計算， 目標價 242
元。 風險因子： 外部 環境變化 、終端需 求、客戶回補庫存 速度 。

投資評等說明  個股評等 Rating 定義 
買進 Buy (B) 相較於市場指數之回報潛力極具吸引力 
增加持股 Overweight (OW) 相較於市場指數之回報潛力略有吸引力 
中立 Neutral (N) 相較於市場指數之回報潛力相似 
降低持股 Underweight (UW) 相較於市場指數之回報潛力稍低 
賣出 Sell (S) 相較於市場指數之回報潛力較低
